def latex_seq(n:int) -> int:
    n = round(n)
    init = [1, 1, 1, 2, 1, 3, 1, 4, 6, 1, 5, 10, 1, 6, 15, 20, 1, 7, 21, 35, 1, 8, 28, 56, 70]
